HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B003","root_000722/B001","root_001069/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0b09c81f91bbd028a896","text":"1. **Forced watering from a source at its heat-limit.** Evidence: `س ق ي` B001 giving drink, B002 providing a watering source; `ع ي ن` B006 springing water-eye; `ء ن ي` B003 reaching due completion/heat limit. Structural cue: this drink follows hot fire and contrasts with 88:12 `عين جارية`. Role: the spring supplies the drink-source, while `آنية` makes it a source whose time/heat has fully arrived. Abductive move: water in punishment can retain the structure of watering while reversing refreshment. Reading change: the ayah reads as anti-saqyā: a complete watering that harms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1549722773e7b6c25cd7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:5:1\",\"root_000722\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"İçirmenin düşmanlığı sürekli biçimde içe yerleştirme çağrışımı, cezayı dış ateşten kişinin içine geçen zorlayıcı bir süreç olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B003","root_000722/B001","root_001069/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2bc24b6e6dab1b521182","text":"1. **A normal act of giving drink is converted into coercive intake at heat's limit.** **Lexical:** `س ق ي` B001, \"giving drink to a drinker\"; `ع ي ن` B006, \"the springing water-eye\"; and `ء ن ي` B003, \"reaching due completion,\" explicitly including a spring whose heat reaches its limit. **Cue:** passive `تُسْقَىٰ` suppresses the drinker's agency, `مِنْ` fixes the source, and the adjective makes the source fully matured in heat after 88:4's fire. **Role:** watering supplies the ordinarily beneficent action, the spring supplies a continuous source, and completed heat reverses the action's expected benefit. **Abduction:** I infer coercion from passive exposure plus punitive surroundings; passive grammar alone does not entail force. **Reading change:** this is not simply \"being given water\" but being made the endpoint of a mature, source-fed heat process.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2f077adfb206f5b76207","text":"The later `عين جارية` made this ayah’s `عين` sharply anti-garden: same source-form, opposite effect. The repeated `كيف` questions made the seeing-eye branch more visible, because the surah trains perception before returning to account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_352b62b913064bcddf70","text":"88:6-7 turned the source into the head of a failed food ecology, while 88:8-10 exposed a contrary ease-producing habitat. That contrast strengthened the irrigation reading; it did not strengthen the exploratory seeing-eye reading, which remains weak because no looking verb enters this fixed window.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B001","root_000063/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_36681acef6b5162f0303","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:5:4\",\"root_000063\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Olgunlaşma ve gecikme çağrışımları, kaynar oluşu yalnız sıcaklık değil, ertelenmiş sürenin ceza olarak vadesine varması şeklinde de gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3f0f0f89d0d6ee578391","text":"4. **Exploratory: the spring's \"eye\" makes the two facial outcomes inspectable.** **Lexical:** `ع ي ن` B001, \"the seeing eye,\" alongside B006, the spring. **Cue:** faces occur before the spring in 88:2 and again after its failed food-chain in 88:8, so the water-eye sits between opposed visible conditions. **Role:** the eye branch supplies a point of observation, while the spring branch supplies the causal-material source. **Abduction:** I infer that the lexical overlap asks the reader to \"see\" outcomes through their sources; this is weak because no looking verb occurs in the focus window. **Reading change:** on this exploratory reading, `عين` is not only water but the aperture through which two bodily ecologies become visibly comparable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B001","root_000063/B001","root_000063/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_41e4f6f40154ffd65ea7","text":"3. **Delay ripens into punishment.** Evidence: `ء ن ي` B001 deliberate delay and B003 due completion; `ء ت ي` B001/B011 arrival of event/calamity in 88:1. Structural cue: the event “has come,” and now the spring is “āniyah.” Role: delayed time reaches its endpoint as consumable punishment. Abductive move: respite or delay is not absence; it matures until the event becomes due. Reading change: `آنية` reads temporally as ripened arrival, not merely boiling quality.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001","root_001520/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7721947977bef341d987","text":"2. **The eye of seeing becomes the eye of drinking.** Evidence: `ع ي ن` B001 seeing eye, B002 face-to-face seeing, B006 water-spring; later `ن ظ ر` B001 looking in 88:17 and repeated `كيف`. Structural cue: the packet repeatedly moves through faces, eyes/springs, and looking. Role: `عين` bridges perception and water-source. Abductive move: the event that should be looked at also returns as a source one must drink from. Reading change: `عين` is not only a spring; it is a visible, confronting source in the visual pedagogy of the surah.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a4c473bf16d91b004046","text":"3. **Watering internalizes hostility rather than relieving need.** **Lexical:** `س ق ي` B010, \"forcing dislike into the heart\" by repeated hostile input. **Cue:** passive watering stands between burning in 88:4 and exclusive, useless food in 88:6-7. **Role:** the B010 variant shifts the action from supplying a liquid to making the subject take harm inward. **Abduction:** I infer the drink's hostile affect from the punitive chain; the fixed ayah describes temperature, not emotion. **Reading change:** `تسقى` can be heard as forced incorporation: punishment crosses from external fire into the subject's interior.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B003","root_001069/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e76d3d441345bf7e8503","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:5:1\",\"root_000722\",[\"B003\"]],[\"88:5:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kaynak, ardından gelen bitki ve giderilmeyen açlıkla birlikte başarısız bir sulama düzeninin başına yerleşir; bol bir başlangıç, aşağıda yalnız zararlı büyüme üretir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B003","root_000722/B001","root_001069/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e7ddf36ba66bbda40d81","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:5:1\",\"root_000722\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:5:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B006\"]],[\"88:5:4\",\"root_000063\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Normalde yararlı olan içirme, öznenin edilgen bırakıldığı ve ısının son sınırına ulaştığı bir kaynaktan zorla içine alınan karşı-beslenmeye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001","root_001069/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e99c4b5168a40fdf4c0f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:5:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kaynağın göz anlamı, önceki ve sonraki karşıt yüzleri sonuçları kaynakları üzerinden görmeye yarayan bir bakış açıklığına dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f5bde7d4c175930fea6f","text":"2. **The spring heads a failed irrigation ecology.** **Lexical:** `س ق ي` B003, \"irrigation share and channel,\" and `ع ي ن` B006, a flowing source. **Cue:** source-water in 88:5 is followed by plant-food in 88:6, explicit failure to fatten or satisfy in 88:7, and then the contrasting ease of 88:8-10. **Role:** the spring functions as upstream supply, while `ضريع` and hunger are downstream biological outputs. **Abduction:** I infer that the spring feeds the plant-system or at least belongs to the same ecology; no irrigation channel is stated. **Reading change:** the ayah becomes the hydraulic head of an anti-provisioning system: abundant sourcing can still generate only hostile growth and unmet need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
